Damage per hit0none
Fire rate0.28 /severy 3 s (0.333 /s), the mini's own interval
Range4 cm4 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Aura reach: 2 cells, centre to centre (diagonals too).
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 4 cm, beside other towers.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.28 shots/s but the mini fires every 3 s (0.333/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never attacks: Update runs it every frame and always tells the engine not to aim or fire it.
  • Every 3 s (the mini's fire interval, read directly) it pulses: the blade snaps, a flash runs to each neighbour and one shot is counted. The pulse changes nothing in play.

Targeting

  • No targeting: it ignores the dudes entirely.

Effects

  • Aura: every placed tower within its AuraReach of 2 cells (twelve tiles: the eight round it and the four two steps out along the axes) gets +30% range.
  • The buff goes into the receiver's AuraRange, which multiplies the reach of its base line and of its card lines.
  • A yellow tape ribbon runs to each tower in reach. It pays out at 2.4 lengths per second and reels in 1.5 times as fast when the neighbour leaves. This is visual only.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Its own line (0 damage) is never read. Cards on it change nothing it does, except support keystones: the engine scales its buff by SupportKeystones.BuffStrength, and SupportKeystoneRules.Reaches can widen or narrow who it reaches.

Quirks

  • It counts toward the engine's cap on how many auras can reach one tower (CardsConfig.AuraCap). A receiver that has hit the cap gets nothing from it.
  • The range gain only helps towers whose code reads api.RangeOf or the mini's range times AuraRange. Towers with a fixed reach in code ignore it (Torch, Third Rail, Toaster Twins, Water Tower, Trebuchet dead zone).
  • A tower with the YOLO! keystone can never be buffed, so it gets nothing.
  • The class summary and the comment on AuraFor say 'the eight tiles round him'. The code reaches twelve tiles (AuraReach 2).

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, AuraFor
  • Engine verbs its code calls: none
  • Named outside its folder in: Game/CoverageReport.cs

ROLLS A D6

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play

Delivery tagsProjectile, Splashwhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape

Force11 cm shove for a grunt from a hit that travels (scaled by weight), if its code strikes with travel
Radius2.1 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 60 cm/s.
  • A hit ends the projectile whatever its Pierce.
  • Bot placement: reach 24 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each throw (and each repeat from cards) rolls its own d6 and lobs a die carrying card damage x the roll (the repeats also x RepeatDamage).
  • Where the die lands (on a dude or on the terrain), a DamageAround of that card damage over 2.1 cm.
  • On a six, a second DamageAround of half that card damage over 5.5 cm, so dudes near the middle are hit twice.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the shot's card damage (Damage stat) as the damage of one pip; Repeats and RepeatDamage give more dice, each with its own roll.
  • Both splash radii are constants (2.1 and 5.5 cm); the line's Radius stat is never read.
  • All damage goes through DamageAround, so it reaches Strike and card on-hit effects apply.

Quirks

  • Damage swings from 1 to 6 x per throw, average 3.5 x.
  • A die can hit a dude in flight (it has a hit radius) and then lands where it is, not on the ground.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, OnProjectileHit, OnProjectileLand, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, Launch
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/Combos/ComboCommands.cs

Tray blurb
Cites a rule against the biggest dude: no armour, no shield, and it takes more from everyone. Page forty-two.
Tray tag line
RULES VS THE BIG ONE

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsPulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit10 hp (x DamageScale 1/1000 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.182 /severy 5.5 s (0.182 /s), the mini's own interval
Range24 cm24 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Bot placement: reach 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Self-driven: every 5.5 s (divided by its aura rate, x0.95 to 1.05) it makes a ruling on its target once it has aimed at it for 0.35 s.
  • A ruling strips the dude's armour and shield and marks it arguing for 4 s (a boss or non-grunt) or 3.5 s (a grunt).
  • It never strikes: no damage at all.

Targeting

  • Keeps its current target while that dude is alive and not arguing, without re-checking range or sight.
  • Otherwise it takes the dude it can see within range with the most hp left (a boss counts 1000 hp more), skipping dudes already arguing.

Effects

  • no-armour and no-shield marks for 4 s or 3.5 s: armour counts for nothing and its shield blocks nothing.
  • A dude marked arguing takes x1.3 from every hit from any tower (DamageTaken).
  • arguing is a decoy mark: the tower earns decoy time for it.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads only the Range stat (api.RangeOf) for choosing a target.
  • Damage, fire rate and Repeats are ignored; fire-rate cards do nothing, only the aura rate shortens the cooldown.
  • It never calls Strike, so no card on-hit effect ever happens from it.

Quirks

  • The summary says it stops the dude in its tracks; nothing holds it (issue 439 made it a ruling). Argue (3) and BossArgue (0.8) are overwritten by 3.5 and 4 s and only appear in the log text.
  • Bosses get the longer mark, not a shorter one.
  • Several Lawyers stack: the engine multiplies each DamageTaken, so two Lawyers give x1.69 on a dude marked arguing.
  • It keeps working while the towers' fire is held: it never asks whether it may shoot.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, DamageTaken
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, RangeOf, StripArmour, StripShield
  • Marks it puts on dudes: arguing, no-armour, no-shield
  • Marks it reads: arguing
  • Named outside its folder in: Game/CcWall.cs

No plan of his own. Copies whichever neighbour is winning. Has opinions about last week.
Tray tag line
COPIES A NEIGHBOUR

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsProjectilewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit40.144 hp (x DamageScale 1/10 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.6 /severy 1.7 s (0.588 /s), the mini's own interval
Range24 cm24 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 22 cm, beside other towers.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • With a neighbour to copy, he fires that tower's first attack line from his own spot (through api.Fire, so its own Fire code runs) at 60% of its card damage, with its Repeats and RepeatDamage.
  • His reload when copying is the source mini's fire interval divided by the source's aura rate and by 0.85, with the usual jitter.
  • With nobody to copy he fires a plain straight bolt of his own line's card damage every mini fire interval (divided by his own aura rate).
  • A ring flashes under the copied tower each shot, and he logs each switch of source.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: he picks the nearest dude he can see, by the sight rules of the tower he copies, with a 0.35 s aim lock; no hunted or boss priority.
  • When copying, his reach is the source mini's range x his own aura range x 0.95; on the starter bolt it is api.RangeOf of his own line.
  • Source choice: of the towers on the four tiles beside him, the one with the most kills this game, ties to the dearer one; towers that are self-driven, stand on the road, are another Meta Chaser, have no card build or have 0 damage on their first line are skipped.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • His own cards matter only for the starter bolt (damage and range).
  • A copied shot uses the source's first line, so the source's cards (damage, on-hit effects, Repeats) apply, at 60% damage; the hit is scaled by the source's DamageScale.
  • Range and fire-rate cards on the source are not read for his reach and reload: he uses the source mini's base range and interval.
  • Kills and XP of copied shots are credited to him, not the source.

Quirks

  • He never checks CanShoot, so he keeps firing during hold-fire and when cards would stop a tower shooting.
  • A copied beam tower (Old Varnish) fires without its wind-up, because Ready is not asked.
  • Copying a Moth Squadron lets out swarms owned by the Squadron itself: they count against its swarm cap and their later chews are credited to it.
  • Every copied shot also adds one to the source's shot count.
  • He swaps source the moment another neighbour has more kills.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Fire, Launch, RangeOf
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On each shot he touches everything in a cone of 48 degrees (full angle) in front of the tip, out to his range plus 1.2 cm.
  • Each dude in the cone that the tip can see first has its armour stripped for 4 s, then takes one Strike of the card damage (base 6) along the aim.

Targeting

  • A dude is in the cone if its chest is within reach (plus half its size) and inside the half-angle widened by its size; a terrain block between tip and chest leaves it out.

Effects

  • Strips armour (StripArmour, the no-armour mark) for 4 s on every dude he touches; every tower's hits on it ignore armour meanwhile.
  • Fire damage type, with DotShare 0.2 in the base card: the burning comes only from the engine's Fire statuses and DoT share, the code sets nothing alight itself.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads f.CardDamage and Range (api.RangeOf, plus 1.2 cm).
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores Repeats, Radius and Pierce.

Quirks

  • The strip comes before his own hit, so his own touch already ignores armour.
  • The counters Burned and Melted are static and never reset; no other code reads them.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Blocked, Marked, RangeOf, Strike, StripArmour
  • Marks it puts on dudes: no-armour
  • Marks it reads: no-armour

Attack

  • A shot clamps jaws on the target. They ride along with it for 2 s while it keeps walking.
  • Then armour and shield are stripped for 0.2 s, and the crush lands: one Strike for the card damage captured at clamp time, as an area hit.
  • If the dude dies before the 2 s are up, the jaws just vanish.
  • If the target is already in any Vice Admiral's jaws, the shot does nothing and the 4 s reload is spent.

Targeting

  • Engine default. It does not skip dudes that are already clamped, so it can keep picking the same one and waste shots.
  • Melee delivery: the engine never picks a floating dude.

Effects

  • At the crush it strips armour (no-armour) and shield (no-shield) for 0.2 s.
  • It marks a newly clamped dude vice-immune for 2 + 3 s. The mark now only decides whether the log and the Clamped counter call it held.
  • Nobody is held or slowed (issue 439).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage, frozen when the jaws close). Range and FireRate come through the engine.
  • Repeats are ignored.
  • The crush goes through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply. It has no direction, so Force gives no knockback.

Quirks

  • The class summary says it 'clamps one enemy in place', slows bosses and holds others. The code holds and slows nobody.
  • If a clamped dude becomes floating before the crush, the Melee Strike does nothing.
  • Several Vice Admirals share one list of jaws, ticked once a frame.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, Strike, StripArmour, StripShield
  • Marks it puts on dudes: no-armour, no-shield, vice-immune
  • Marks it reads: vice-immune
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

CUTS A WHOLE LINE

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Bot placement: reach 24 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire it throws a disc in a straight line from the muzzle out to its reach at 55 cm/s, then the disc flies back to where it started.
  • It aims down the line with the most dudes on it: for every dude in reach it counts how many chests lie within 1.8 x size of the full-length line towards that dude, and takes the best.
  • Every dude the disc passes within 1.8 x its size of is struck once on the way out and can be struck once more on the way back, each time for the full card damage.

Targeting

  • The engine's target only matters if no dude is in reach for the line count.
  • No sight check for the aim or the cuts: the disc flies through terrain.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage is f.CardDamage per cut; reach is api.RangeOf with a 6 cm floor.
  • Repeats and Pierce are ignored: one disc per fire, and it always passes through everything.
  • Cuts go through Strike travelling along the disc's direction (reversed on the way back), so card on-hit effects and Force cards apply.

Quirks

  • Several discs can be in the air at once (a throw takes about 2 x reach / 55 s, less than the reload).
  • If the tower is lifted or sold while the disc is out, the disc vanishes once it turns round.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: RangeOf, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never attacks. Every 3 s (the mini's interval) it plays its pose and flashes each neighbour; this changes nothing.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: no aim and no target.

Effects

  • Aura on the eight tiles round it: +20% damage and +10% range; no fire rate.
  • Every hit any tower makes on a dude standing on a slope within its reach is multiplied by 1.3 (+30%).
  • A slope is ground under the dude's position rising more than 0.2 cm per cm, measured 1 cm either side.
  • Its reach for the slope bonus is the mini's range (4 cells) times its own range aura, measured flat; no line of sight is needed.
  • The first slope hit on a dude lays the mark level-glint for 0.5 s (only a flash).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads no line stats: Range cards do not widen the slope zone (it uses the mini's range, not RangeOf), and Damage cards change nothing.

Quirks

  • The blurb says slopes 'in sight'; the code checks only distance.
  • Two Spirit Levels covering the same dude multiply their bonuses.
  • The aura's damage bonus does not apply to splash hits (DamageAround), an engine rule.
  • The static counter SlopeHits is never reset; no other code reads it.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotAdded, Update, AuraFor, DamageTaken
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked

Swallows one small enemy whole, then needs a long sit down. Mind the big ones.
Tray tag line
EATS ONE SMALL

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsMeleewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit140.42 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.7 /severy 1.4 s (0.714 /s), the mini's own interval
Range11.5 cm11.5 cm, the mini's own range
Force22 cm shove for a grunt from a hit that travels (scaled by weight), if its code strikes with travel
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 11.5 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • If the target is small, it swallows it. Small means: not a boss, grunt tier, base hp at most 3.5, scale at most 1.25, and not Armoured or Shielded.
  • Swallow: the dude is held 0.6 s. After 0.22 s it is removed as a kill credited to the trap (api.Remove), and the trap gets XP for its current hp.
  • Swallow, then: the trap is busy digesting for min(14, 3 + 1.8 x current hp) seconds, plus 0.3 s.
  • Otherwise it bites: one Strike for the line's card damage, aimed from the muzzle (so the line's Force of 2 knocks it back).
  • While digesting it does nothing at all: Ready is false and Fire returns at once.

Targeting

  • Engine default. It takes whatever the engine picks and decides at fire time whether to swallow or bite, so it may bite a big dude even with small ones in reach.
  • Melee delivery: the engine never picks a floating dude for it.

Effects

  • A 0.6 s hold on a dude it swallows (glue rules apply).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • A bite reads Damage (f.CardDamage) and goes through Strike, so card on-hit effects and Force apply.
  • A swallow ignores the damage stat and bypasses Strike: no card on-hit hooks run, though the kill counts for the trap.
  • Repeats are ignored. Range and FireRate come through the engine.

Quirks

  • A swallow kills regardless of hp, but 'small' is judged on the enemy type's base hp, not its current hp. Late-wave health growth only makes digestion longer.
  • If another tower kills the prey during the 0.22 s snap, the digestion is cut to at most 0.4 s.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Ready, Fire, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Hold, Remove, Strike
  • Shared rules it reads: Balance.Damage
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

SOAKS, GROWS BRAMBLES

Range18 cm18 cm, the mini's own range
Radius3.4 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Projectile (default Fire): lobbed shell, hit radius 0 cm round the chest.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 60 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Bot placement: reach 16 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Default lob: one drop per shot (plus one per card Repeat).
  • On landing it does a DamageAround splash of radius max(2, the line's Radius; base 3.4 cm) for the card damage, with falloff.
  • It marks every dude in that radius (flat, plus half its scale) wet for 6 s.
  • It grows a moss patch at the target's current position if the target is alive, else where the drop landed.
  • Twice a second, while it is placed, every dude within 3.6 cm (flat) of any of its moss patches takes 0.3 x the line's card damage through Strike.

Effects

  • DamageTaken: while a dude carries the wet mark (from any source), every hit on it from any tower, this one included, does +15%.
  • Moss patch: radius 3.6 cm, lasts 30 s, at most 5 per can (the oldest goes). A drop near an existing patch only refreshes it.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage for the splash, line.Stats[Stat.Damage] x 0.3 for each moss tick), Radius (splash and soak only, minimum 2 cm), Repeats, and Range and FireRate through the engine.
  • The moss radius and life are constants.
  • Splash and moss hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply, but the hits have no direction, so Force does nothing.
  • The splash is DamageAround, so a damage aura does not boost it. The moss ticks are not a splash, so they are boosted.

Quirks

  • The bonus stacks between cans: each Watering Can in play multiplies a wet dude's damage by 1.15.
  • Any wet mark counts: Water Balloon, Water Tower, Shower Head, and the combo engine (which marks what a Wet-affinity tower hits while a combo is active) all feed it.
  • The class summary says the soak is a short slow and the moss slows. The code slows nobody: the moss does tick damage instead (issue 439). MossSlow and BossMossSlow are unused.
  • The tip says 'GROWS BRAMBLES'. The code calls it moss and it only does damage.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, OnProjectileLand, DamageTaken, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, Mark, Marked, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: wet
  • Marks it reads: wet
  • Named outside its folder in: Game/CcWall.cs

Tray blurb
A prism on a string. Where the window's light lies across the sill, one beam becomes three.
Tray tag line
BEAM SPLITS IN LIGHT

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typeArcaneno status of its own
Delivery tagsBeamwhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit302.7 hp (x DamageScale 1/4 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.45 /severy 2.2 s (0.455 /s), the mini's own interval
Range30 cm30 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Beam: an instant hit once its mini is charged.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Bot placement: reach 24 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • A beam tower: after a 0.7 s wind-up it fires one beam along the aim, out to its range or the first terrain in the way.
  • It looks along the beam in 0.5 cm steps (from 1.5 cm out to 2 cm before the end) for the first point inside a stripe of light.
  • No stripe on the way: one beam whose first 1 + Pierce dudes along it (within 1.1 x their size) each take 60% of the card damage.
  • A stripe on the way: the stretch up to it hits the same way at 60%, then the beam splits into three at 16 degrees apart.
  • Each of the three split beams runs on for the rest of the range (at least 8 cm) and hits the first dude on it not hit already, at 75% of the card damage.

Effects

  • The stripes: bands 9.5 cm wide every 24 cm, slanted at 20 degrees, fixed in table coordinates; they are drawn while a Sun Catcher is in play.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads f.CardDamage, Range (api.RangeOf) and Pierce (line.Stats[Stat.Pierce], more dudes on the unsplit stretch only).
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores Repeats: one beam per shot.

Quirks

  • No beam ever does full card damage: unsplit hits do 60% and split hits 75%. The summary says light does full damage.
  • Each split beam hits one dude at most, whatever the Pierce.
  • The beams pick dudes along the line with no sight check of their own; only the beam's end is cut by terrain.
  • The stripes are worked out from position alone, so they exist on every table, not only the sill.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotAdded, OnSlotRemoved, Update, Ready, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: AlongLine, RangeOf, Strike, Sweep
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 9 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.45 shots/s but the mini fires every 2.8 s (0.357/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On a shot, every light dude within its range (flat, plus half its scale) takes the full card damage through Strike, as an area hit.
  • Light means: not a boss, grunt tier, base hp at most 3.5, scale at most 1.25.
  • Heavy dudes are skipped entirely.

Targeting

  • It only fires while a light dude is within 11 cm x its aura range of its base (flat). Otherwise Update stops the engine aiming or firing it.
  • The engine still picks the target, which may be a heavy dude, but the target does not matter: the pulse hits every light dude in range.
  • No sight check for the dudes it hits.

Effects

  • None beyond the damage. No shove and no stagger: the code works out a sideways direction but never uses it.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage) and Range (api.RangeOf for the hit radius). FireRate comes through the engine.
  • Repeats are ignored.
  • Hits go through Strike with no direction, so card on-hit effects apply but the Force of 3 gives no knockback.

Quirks

  • The class summary says it shoves light dudes across the road and staggers them for Stun (0.8) s. The code does neither, and Stun and ShoveLane are unused.
  • The fire gate uses 11 cm x aura range, but the hit uses api.RangeOf (range cards and the run's range multiplier too). With range cards it can hit farther than the distance that lets it ring.

Home table

  • On its home window sill, every gust of the sill wind (one every 11 s) makes each placed chime that can shoot ring once for free, at the line's full card damage, and say 'GUST'. Its reload is untouched.
  • A gust ring happens even when no light dude is in reach, in which case it hits nothing.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: CanShoot, Fire, OnHomeTable, RangeOf, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

CHEWS, THEN HOPS

Card unitsIn play

Range22 cm22 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 30 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each shot lets out a swarm of moths at the engine's target, unless this tower already has 2 swarms out (then the shot does nothing).
  • The swarm flies at 32 cm/s, ignoring walls, to the target's chest.
  • Within 1.2 cm it chews: one Strike of the line's card damage every 0.5 s.
  • After 4 s on one dude, or when it dies, the swarm hops to the nearest living dude within 14 cm of the swarm, at most 3 times; then it goes home.
  • A swarm also goes home after 16 s in total, or when nobody is within 14 cm.

Targeting

  • Only the first dude is the engine's target; each hop goes to the nearest living dude to the swarm, ignoring sight, bosses and range.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Chew damage is the line's Damage stat read when the swarm was let out, through Strike with no travel direction, so on-hit cards and DoT apply on every chew.
  • Repeats are ignored; FireRate only sets how often a new swarm can go out; Range only matters for the first target.

Quirks

  • The summary comment promises a slight slow; the code has none.
  • Swarms are removed when the tower leaves the game.
  • Up to 1 + 3 dudes per swarm, and a long-lived dude is left after 4 s even if alive.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

Targeting

  • Radius cards widen the circle (api.RadiusOf); Range cards only change when it fires.
  • Repeats are ignored: one flash per shot.

  • It no longer stops or staggers anyone (issue 439 comment): the summary's stare and boss stagger are not in the code, and the consts Stare (1.3) and Dazzle (4) are unused.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each shot strikes the target with the full card damage, then jumps to the nearest unhit living dude within 9 cm of the last one, up to 4 jumps (five dudes in all).
  • Each link does 0.85 x the one before (85%).
  • Every link goes through Strike, in the direction it travelled.

Targeting

  • Jumps ignore sight and the tower's range: only distance from the last dude counts.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage via f.CardDamage and Strike per link, so on-hit effects and the base burn (Fire damage, DotShare 0.4 in the stat block) apply to every link.
  • The jump range and count are consts: Radius, Pierce and Repeats are not read by its code.

Quirks

  • A jump can go back towards the tower or to dudes far out of its range, as long as they are within 9 cm of the last one hit.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Engine verbs its code calls: Strike

Says boo from under a dust sheet. Everyone in earshot is haunted and takes more from every tower. Slow to get going.

HAUNTS THE GROUP

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typeArcaneno status of its own
Delivery tagsPulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit30.27 hp (x DamageScale 1/4 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.16 /severy 7 s (0.143 /s), the mini's own interval
Range18 cm18 cm, the mini's own range
Radius10 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Field: radius 10 cm (aura keystones read it).
  • Bot placement: reach 16 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.16 shots/s but the mini fires every 7 s (0.143/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each fire says boo round the target: every living dude within the radius plus half its scale of the target's position, measured on the flat.
  • The radius is the line's Radius stat (at least 6 cm) times the support keystone field scale (Deep Mark, Wide Field).
  • Each dude in the circle is marked ghost-scared for 5 s and takes one Strike of the shot's card damage with no travel direction (power 0.3).

Effects

  • While a dude carries ghost-scared, every hit on it from any tower is x1.25 (+25%). Big and small dudes alike.
  • As an aura keystone field: InField is the ghost-scared mark, FieldTakes is x1.25, field radius 10 cm, reach 18 cm plus 10 x the field scale.
  • ghost-scared is a decoy mark: the ghost earns decoy time while it lasts.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the shot's card damage (Damage stat) and the line's Radius stat (via RadiusOf, at least 6 cm); Repeats are ignored.
  • The boo goes through Strike, so card on-hit effects and the damage type apply.

Quirks

  • The summary says grunts turn round and run back and bosses only flinch; the code turns nobody round. Every dude in the circle is only marked and struck (issue 439 comment), big or small.
  • The 3 s 'brave' immunity in the summary is not in the code: a dude can be marked again by the very next boo.
  • The x1.25 is applied once per ghost in play: with two ghosts placed, a scared dude takes x1.25 twice, whichever ghost scared it.
  • The DamageScale comment says the boo is 2 card points; the base card is 3.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, DamageTaken, InField, FieldTakes, FieldReach, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, RadiusOf, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: ghost-scared
  • Marks it reads: ghost-scared
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire it hits a flat cone 60 degrees wide (widened by each dude's size) along its aim, out to its range from the muzzle.
  • Every dude in the cone that the muzzle can see (terrain not in the way) is struck once for the full card damage.

Targeting

  • Direct: a flock patch hides its target from it.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage is f.CardDamage; reach is api.RangeOf, so range cards and auras lengthen the cone.
  • The Radius stat (6 on the base card) and Repeats are ignored.
  • Hits go through Strike with no direction: card on-hit effects apply, Force does nothing, shields never block.

Quirks

  • The summary says the cone shoves everything back a step; the code shoves nobody. ShoveCm (36) and BigShoveK (0.4) are declared but never used.

Thumps on the beat. Everything in the ring hops and lands hard. Put it where they bunch. Thumps with the band's kick.
Tray tag line
HOP, THUD

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsSplash, Pulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit200.6 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.5 /severy 2 s (0.5 /s), the mini's own interval
Range11 cm11 cm, the mini's own range
Radius11 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Stands on the road as well as on build tiles.
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 9 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On each beat it takes every living dude whose flat distance from the drum is within its range plus half the dude's scale, whatever the engine's target and whatever stands in the way.
  • Each of them hops: the model is lifted in an arc for 0.4 s (0.4 x 0.7 s for a boss or mini-boss) while the walker keeps walking underneath.
  • When the hop ends the dude takes one Strike of the full card damage, with no travel direction (an area hit) and no falloff.
  • Two rings flash on the beat (the second 0.12 s later at 70% of the radius); the number of hops is logged as [kick-drum].

Targeting

  • The engine target only decides when it fires; the hit goes to everyone in the ring, so sight and flock patches do not matter (Direct is false).
  • It may stand on the road itself.

Effects

  • The hop is only a lift of the model: an ordinary dude rises 3.2 cm, a heavy grunt (6 hp or more, or scale 1.3 or more) 30% of that, a boss or mini-boss 12%.
  • No hold, slow or shove is applied by its own code.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Ring radius is api.RangeOf (the Range stat, auras and run multiplier); the Radius stat is not read.
  • Damage is f.CardDamage, dealt through Strike, so on-hit cards, damage type statuses and DoT apply on landing.
  • Repeats are ignored: one hop per dude per beat.
  • Its delivery has Splash, so a Captain Lightbox gives it only half the fire-rate bonus; the damage bonus does apply (its hits are not DamageAround).

Quirks

  • The code works out a slow after landing (55% speed for 1.3 s, heavier dudes less) and stores it on each hop, but Land never applies it: the slow promised in the summary comment and the Blurb does not happen.
  • w.Boss is true for every tier above grunt, so mini-bosses get the boss height, air time and (unused) slow values.
  • The damage lands 0.4 s after the beat; a dude that dies in the air is dropped from the list and takes nothing.
  • A dude caught by two beats (two drums, or a quick second beat) has two hops at once; both lifts add to its model height.

Home table

  • On the garage, a drum on its home table listens to the garage drum machine: on each thump, every drum that is placed, can shoot, has a live target and is at least 75% reloaded fires at once.
  • If its next beat comes within 5 frames of that forced fire, it pops 'ON THE BEAT' over the drum.
  • When no drum is left in the garage it stops listening; the next drum placed listens again.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, CanShoot, OnHomeTable, RangeOf, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On the engine's fire, if no loop is out and it is not coiling, it throws a loop onto the target's road 2.6 cm ahead of the target (at most 3 cm before the road's end).
  • The loop works once it has landed (0.28 s): every dude on that route within 0.8 cm along the road of it, without the mark tangled, is caught.
  • A caught dude gets the mark tangled for 8 s and one Strike of the line's card damage (base 15), with no direction.
  • The loop is spent after 3 dudes or 7 s; then it coils for 2 s before it can throw again.

Targeting

  • Ready is false while the loop is out or coiling.
  • Dudes with the mark tangled pass the loop untouched.

Effects

  • Mark tangled for 8 s, one of the engine's decoy marks, so it earns decoy time.
  • No hold, trip or knock-down (issue 439): the dudes keep walking.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads only the line's Damage (line.Stats[Stat.Damage]); the hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores Repeats, Radius (base 2) and Force; Range only matters for the engine's targeting.

Quirks

  • The summary says dudes trip and lie flat (1.6 s, big 0.8 s, boss 0.45 s); the code works the time out and never uses it, and no fall is drawn.
  • Bosses are caught and hit like anyone else.
  • The loop is not limited to its range: it lands 2.6 cm ahead of the target wherever that is.
  • Only dudes on the loop's own route count.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Ready, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: tangled
  • Marks it reads: tangled
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Field: radius 3.2 cm (aura keystones read it).
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 9 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never attacks. A spot of light sweeps to and fro along one road, 5.5 cm each side of a centre point, one there-and-back every 5.5 s.
  • On the mini's fire interval it plays a strobe pose; this changes nothing.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: no aim and no target.
  • The swept stretch is centred on the point of any route nearest the tower (kept 5.5 + 1 cm from the road's ends), chosen again only when the tower moves.

Effects

  • A dude within 3.2 cm (flat, plus 0.4 x its scale) of the spot's centre is lit: mark lit for 0.3 s, renewed every frame, and a ring at its feet.
  • Every hit from any tower other than a Stage Light on a lit dude is multiplied by 1.3 (+30%).
  • Two Stage Lights over the same dude count once: only the one with the lowest slot index gives the bonus.
  • It is an aura-keystone field: Deep Mark and Wide Field scale the spot's radius, Spotlight slows dudes in it, Lingering Mark keeps the x1.3 after they leave.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads no line stats: Range, Damage and FireRate cards change nothing; only keystones that scale fields change the spot.

Quirks

  • There is no range check: the spot lies on the nearest road however far away it is.
  • It stacks with other damage-taken towers (a Spirit Level, a Night Light) by multiplication; only other Stage Lights do not stack.
  • It shares the lit mark key with the Night Light.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, DamageTaken, InField, FieldTakes, FieldReach
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark
  • Marks it puts on dudes: lit

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

Targeting

Effects

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

Quirks

  • It slows nobody: GruntSlow (0.62) and BigSlow (0.88) are declared, and the summary promises a slow, but there is no SpeedFactor in the code.

  • It burns and strips shields even while the towers are held from firing.
  • The smoked mark is read by nothing else.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each fire marks a red patch of radius 5.2 cm on the ground at the point on the target's route where it will be: its speed x 0.95 s ahead, at most 7 cm.
  • The slab starts to drop 0.26 s before the hit and lands 0.95 s after the fire.
  • On landing, every living dude within 5.2 cm plus 0.4 x its size of the patch centre takes one Strike of the shot's card damage, directed away from the tower (power 1.1).
  • The landing counts as the shot.

Targeting

  • The patch hits every dude in it, on any road and any side, not only the target; it is aimed at a predicted point, so a dude that stops or turns can be missed.

Effects

  • Dudes in the patch that are not marked flat-cool get the flattened mark for 1.5 s and flat-cool for 3.5 s.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the shot's card damage (Damage stat) and the target's speed; the patch radius is the constant 5.2, so the line's Radius stat is never read. Repeats are ignored.
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply and the line's Force knocks dudes away from the tower.

Quirks

  • Nothing in the game reads the flattened mark: the stagger the summary promises (and 0.5 s for a big one) does not happen. Flattening is only a mark.
  • Dudes still on flat-cool take the damage, they just are not marked again.
  • Lifting the tower before the slab lands cancels the hit.
  • The comment on its base card says 30 card damage; the base card says 45.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, Strike

  • Marks it reads: flat-cool

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each fire lays a red line on the ground from 2.4 cm in front of the bottle along the flat direction to the target, past it by 5 cm: length clamped between 6 cm and range + 5 cm. It grows to full length over 0.4 s.
  • When laid, the first 8 dudes within 1.4 x their size of the line (at the target's chest height) are marked ketchup for 3 s and take one Strike of the shot's card damage along the line (power 0.3).
  • Every 0.5 s, each dude standing on any of this bottle's lines takes one Strike of 0.7 x the line's Damage stat, with no travel direction.
  • A line lasts 9 s; the bottle keeps at most 3, a new one replaces the oldest.

Targeting

  • The line lies where it was squeezed, across any road; it hits any dude on it, from any route.

Effects

  • A dude on a line is marked ketchup for 1.6 s; a dude carrying ketchup leaves a footprint (radius 0.95 cm, 3.5 s) at most every 0.4 s (foot-cool), up to 26 per bottle.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the shot's card damage when laid, the line's Damage stat for the ticks, and Range via RangeOf for the line's length. The line width is the constant 2.2; the line's Radius stat is never read. Repeats are ignored.
  • All hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.

Quirks

  • Nothing slows: the summary's 55% on the line, 85% for big dudes and 80% in footprints are constants nothing uses. Footprints are only a picture (issue 439 changed the line to damage ticks).
  • The Blurb's burn is plain hits of the line's damage type; the code adds no burn status of its own.
  • A dude standing on two lines still takes one tick per 0.5 s.
  • Lifting the bottle clears its lines.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: AlongLine, Mark, Marked, RangeOf, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: foot-cool, ketchup
  • Marks it reads: foot-cool, ketchup
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

Smiles at the horde until it swings at him. Distracted dudes take more from every other tower. Cannot be broken.
Tray tag line
DECOY: EXPOSES SMALL

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play

Delivery tagsPulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape

Fire rate1 /severy 0.6 s (1.67 /s), the mini's own interval

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Field: radius 19 cm (aura keystones read it).
  • Bot placement: reach 24 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 1 shots/s but the mini fires every 0.6 s (1.67/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 24 cm; its mini reaches 19 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • It never fires or deals damage. Every frame it takes on each new small dude within reach (any side, measured on the flat): marks it gnome-busy for 3.5 s and gnome-bored for 3.5 + 5 s.
  • The reach is 19 cm times the support keystone field scale (Deep Mark, Wide Field).
  • Each new taunt counts as a shot; every 0.55 s while anyone is busy the gnome plays its hit animation and the busy dudes flinch.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it. It takes at most 5 busy dudes at a time, counting every dude marked gnome-busy within reach + 2 cm, whichever gnome marked it.
  • It skips big dudes (bosses and every non-grunt tier), dudes already busy, and dudes still gnome-bored. No sight check.
  • The mini looks at the nearest busy dude.

Effects

  • Every hit from any tower on a dude marked gnome-busy is x1.35 (+35%).
  • As an aura keystone field: InField is the gnome-busy mark, FieldTakes is x1.35, field radius 19 cm.
  • gnome-busy is a decoy mark: the gnome earns decoy time while it lasts.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Its code reads nothing from its attack line: no Damage, Range, Radius or FireRate, so cards that change them do nothing; only the support keystones' field scale changes its reach.
  • It never calls Strike, so card on-hit effects never fire from it. It never asks CanShoot either.

Quirks

  • The summary says busy dudes stand still for 2.4 s; the code never holds or slows them. They keep walking, flinch, and take +35% more.
  • The numbers in its comments are stale: busy lasts 3.5 s (comment 2.4 s), the re-taunt wait is 5 s after that (comment six seconds), the bonus is x1.35 (comments a fifth and 20%), and there is no one-taunt-a-second limit.
  • The x1.35 is applied once per gnome in play: with two gnomes placed, a busy dude takes x1.35 twice, wherever it is.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, DamageTaken, InField, FieldTakes
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked
  • Marks it puts on dudes: gnome-bored, gnome-busy
  • Marks it reads: gnome-bored, gnome-busy
  • Named outside its folder in: Game/CcWall.cs

Scribes a circle of pencil round himself. Whoever walks over the line is cut. Put him where the road runs through.

CUTS WHAT CROSSES

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsMelee, Pulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit90.27 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.385 /severy 2.6 s (0.385 /s), the mini's own interval
Range9 cm9 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 9 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never aims. It draws a circle on the table round itself; radius is the line's range (api.RangeOf, at least 3 cm; base 9 cm).
  • The first full circle takes 2.6 s to draw; only the drawn part cuts. Once drawn, the whole circle stays live.
  • Each dude that crosses the drawn line, in or out, is struck with the line's Damage stat through Strike (base 9), in its walking direction.
  • The same dude cannot be cut again by the same compass for 0.4 s (mark cut plus the slot index).

Targeting

  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it.
  • A move of more than 3 cm across the line in one frame (a teleport or a throw) does not count as a crossing.

Effects

  • Each cut leaves a pencil tick on the road for 20 s (at most 36 ticks kept), and marks the dude pencilled for 4 s; nothing reads that mark.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Range cards widen the circle (api.RangeOf); Damage cards change the cut (line.Stats[Damage]); every cut goes through Strike, so on-hit effects apply.
  • The drawing speed scales with the fire-rate aura (at least 0.5 x) but not with FireRate cards. Repeats and Radius are not read.

Quirks

  • Its line is Melee, so Strike skips floating dudes: they cross the line uncut.
  • It keeps cutting while the towers are held from firing.
  • Lifting it resets the drawing: it must draw the circle again after being moved.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, RangeOf, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: cut, pencilled
  • Marks it reads: cut

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 8 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.154 shots/s but the mini fires every 5 s (0.2/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 8 cm; its mini reaches 14 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Self-driven. When placed it stretches a band from its hook across the nearest road point (at least 2.5 cm from the tower) to a pin 3.2 cm past it; it has no band if that point is out of its range.
  • While taut, every dude whose flat distance to the band is under 0.95 + 0.2 x its size leans on it.
  • It flicks when someone leans and either the lean has lasted 1 s, or the leaners plus the dudes in the 9 cm of road behind the band weigh 3 or more (a heavy dude counts 2) and the lean has lasted 0.3 s.
  • A flick strikes every dude on that road from 9 cm behind the band to 1.2 cm past it, plus all leaners, for the line's card damage, the hit travelling back up the road.
  • Each survivor is then pushed back 80 push units (about 11 cm of road before diminishing returns) and staggered for 0.6 s.
  • After a flick the band hangs slack for 5.5 s, then is taut again.

Targeting

  • Never uses the engine's targeting; the band's place decides everything.
  • Leaners are found by distance to the band only, on any route; the flick zone is on the band's own route.
  • Heavy means a boss, any non-grunt tier, or an enemy with 6 or more hp or scale 1.3 or more.

Effects

  • Leaning slows a dude to 0.3 of its speed (a boss to 0.7) while the band is taut.
  • Push of 80 units (Push divides by 7, so about 11 cm, cut by the engine's diminishing shoves) and a 0.6 s stagger on every flicked dude.
  • The smack also knocks back through Strike: its travel is back up the road and the line has Knockback delivery and Force 2.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Smack damage reads the line's Damage stat (6 on the base card); reach uses api.RangeOf, so range cards let it span a road further away.
  • The fire rate stat is ignored: the cycle is fixed by 1 and 5.5.
  • Repeats and Radius are ignored.
  • The smack goes through Strike, so card on-hit effects and Force apply.

Quirks

  • A lone dude is flicked too, once it has leaned 1 s.
  • It keeps working while the towers' fire is held: it never asks whether it may shoot.
  • Re-aims the band only when the tower moves more than 1 cm.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, SpeedFactor
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Push, RangeOf, Stagger, Strike

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each fire lays a line of glue across the target's route 3.5 cm ahead of the lead dude, at most 3 cm before the route's end; if that point is out of reach, 1.5 cm ahead instead.
  • Every frame, the first 4 dudes on that route between 0.3 cm before and 1.1 cm after the line are caught, one each, whatever their place across the road.
  • Each catch makes one Strike of the line's card damage with no travel direction (power 0.4).
  • When 4 are caught or 10 s pass, the line is spent and the stick winds for 4.5 s; it cannot fire while a line is down or while it winds.

Targeting

  • The engine picks the target; the code then takes the lead dude, the living dude furthest along the target's route within range of the tower (no sight check).
  • A dude caught by this stick is marked glued plus the tower's slot number for 10 + 1 s and is not caught again by its lines in that time.

Effects

  • Hold: 1.6 s for a small dude (engine glue rules apply).
  • Slow: a heavy dude (boss, non-grunt tier, 6 or more hit points, or scale 1.3 or more) is slowed to 45% speed for 2.2 s instead of held.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the line's Damage stat for each catch and Range via RangeOf for the lead dude; Radius, Repeats and Pierce are never read.
  • The code's own hold is the constant 1.6; the line's StickSecs stat (set to 1.6 on its base card) also lands as a Stuck status through Strike, so cards that raise StickSecs lengthen that.
  • Catches go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.

Quirks

  • Because Strike carries the line's StickSecs, a heavy dude that the code only means to slow also gets the engine's hold from the hit, under the glue rules (bosses resist).
  • Only dudes on the same route as the target are caught; a dude on another route crossing the same spot walks over the line.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Ready, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Hold, Mark, Marked, RangeOf, Slow, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: glued
  • Marks it reads: glued

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never shoots. It shows a digit from 1 to 9 and changes it every 6 s to a new digit, never the same twice in a row.
  • The screen flickers for the last 1.3 s before each change.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it.

Effects

  • Aura: each tower on the four tiles beside it (AuraReach 1; six on hexes) gets damage + digit x 0.04 (+4% per point, 4 to 36%).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Its code reads no line stats: the Period (6) s clock is a const, so FireRate cards and auras do not speed it up.

Quirks

  • The first change comes 3.6 to 6 s after it is first placed (a random head start of up to 0.4 x 6); the clock runs only while placed.
  • The digits come from a per-tower seeded random sequence (seeded by slot index).
  • An aura damage bonus does not apply to splash hits made with DamageAround, but it does apply to the Cannon's blast, which strikes directly.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, AuraFor
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After

  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 60 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 17 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.3 shots/s but the mini fires every 3.6 s (0.278/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Lobs a squirt at the point on the target's route 5 cm ahead of it, with a flight time of distance / 45 clamped to 0.4-0.9 s.
  • Where it lands, a DamageAround of the shot's card damage over 3.3 cm.
  • Then a puddle of radius 3.3 cm for 11 s; a squirt landing within about 0.7 x 3.3 cm of an existing puddle only freshens it (its age drops to at most 1.5 s).
  • Three wasps live round the box. Each picks the slowest dude standing in a puddle, flies to it (top speed 38 cm/s) and, within 1.6 cm, chews it every 0.9 s: one Strike of 0.8 x the oldest puddle's card damage, no travel direction.

Targeting

  • The wasps' 'slowest' is a dude held or frozen first, then the lowest slow share from a slow status, then the one furthest back on its road; the puddle's own slow does not count.

Effects

  • Slow: a dude in any of this box's puddles moves at 55% speed; a boss or any non-grunt tier at 85%.
  • At most 3 puddles per box; a new one removes the oldest.
  • Each puddle is noted as a control zone for 11 s, so the box earns control time.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the shot's card damage (Damage stat) for the landing splash and the chews; the puddle radius is the constant 3.3, so the line's Radius stat is never read. Repeats are ignored.
  • The landing splash and the chews go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.

Quirks

  • Each chew takes its damage, owner and line from the oldest puddle, not the one the dude stands in.
  • Puddles do not age and the wasps do not fly while the box is lifted.
  • The comment on its base card says a puddle every 3.3 s; the mini fires every 3.6 s.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Fire, OnProjectileLand, SpeedFactor, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, IsHeld, Launch, NoteZone, Strike

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire it picks the dude furthest along its road within reach of the muzzle (plus half its size) and in sight, skipping dudes marked buckle-spent or carseat-control; if there is none it uses the engine's target.
  • It strikes that dude for the full card damage.
  • If the dude survives and is not buckle-spent or carseat-control, a grunt is held 2 s and a non-grunt non-boss 1.3 s, and marked buckled for that time.
  • It is also marked buckle-spent and carseat-control for the hold plus 4 s.
  • A boss is not held: it is staggered 0.6 s and slowed to 0.4 speed for 1.2 s, marked buckle-spent for 4 s and carseat-control for 0.6 + 4 s.

Targeting

  • Its own pick: the front-runner by distance along its road (compared across routes too), not the engine's nearest dude.

Effects

  • Hold 2 s (grunt) or 1.3 s (non-grunt), under the engine's glue grip rules.
  • A buckled dude takes x1.25 from every hit (+25%); several Buckles do not stack (only the lowest-numbered one answers).
  • carseat-control is shared with the Air Freshener: a dude either of them controlled is skipped by both.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage is f.CardDamage; reach is api.RangeOf.
  • Repeats and Radius are ignored; hold times are fixed constants.
  • The click goes through Strike travelling from the muzzle to the dude, credited to the copying tower if any, so card on-hit effects, the line's Stagger delivery and Force apply.

Quirks

  • When every dude in reach is spent, the engine's target is clicked for damage only.
  • A boss is slowed as well as staggered, which the summary does not say.

Home table

  • On the car seat: 2.6 s after any announcement containing BRAKE or EXIT (the drive's brake warnings), every buckled dude is let go at once.
  • Letting go ends the dude's whole hold, including glue or holds from other towers.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Fire, DamageTaken
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Blocked, Hold, Mark, Marked, RangeOf, Slow, Stagger, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: buckle-spent, buckled, carseat-control
  • Marks it reads: buckle-spent, buckled, carseat-control

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • It lobs a scent canister at the road point 4 cm ahead of its target along the target's route, with a flight time of 0.4 to 0.9 s (distance / 45).
  • Where it lands it splashes card damage with DamageAround over 3.8 cm.
  • It then lays a scent cloud on the nearest point of any route to the landing spot (searched in 1.5 cm steps), wherever the canister actually landed.
  • The cloud lasts 5.5 s and drifts back along its road towards the entrance at 2.2 cm/s (never below 1 cm along the route).
  • Each tower keeps at most 2 clouds; a third one removes the oldest.

Effects

  • A dude within 3.8 cm (flat distance) of a cloud's centre is turned round: mark scent for 1.5 s, during which its speed factor is -0.7 (it walks backwards).
  • A mini-boss or other non-grunt is turned for 0.9 s at speed factor -0.4 (mark scent-big).
  • A boss is not turned: it is slowed to 50% speed for 1 s (mark scent-boss).
  • On being turned it gets scent-spent for the turn time plus 4 s, and cannot be turned again by any cloud while it lasts.
  • It also gets the car-seat control mark carseat-control (SeatBeltBuckleSpec.Controlled) for the same time; a dude already carrying it, for example from the Seat Belt Buckle, is ignored by the cloud.
  • The tower earns control time for the cloud (NoteZone over 3.8 cm for 5.5 s).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • The landing splash uses f.CardDamage through DamageAround, so Damage cards and on-hit effects apply to the puff only; the cloud itself deals no damage.
  • The splash and cloud radius is the const CloudRadius (3.8), not the line's Radius stat, so Radius cards do not change it.
  • Repeats are ignored: one canister per shot. Range comes from the mini and engine targeting.

Quirks

  • The cloud keeps working while the tower is held from firing; only its puffs need a shot.
  • Because the turn is a negative speed factor, it beats any other slow on the same dude (the smallest factor wins).
  • The cloud does not check which road a dude is on, only flat distance to the cloud centre.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Fire, OnProjectileLand, SpeedFactor, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, Launch, Mark, Marked, NoteZone
  • Marks it puts on dudes: scent, scent-big, scent-boss, scent-spent
  • Marks it reads: scent, scent-big, scent-boss, scent-spent

MARKS A STRETCH

Card unitsIn play

Range24 cm24 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Field: radius 9 cm (aura keystones read it).
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 18 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never strikes. It runs its own clock: a page is open for 6 s, then the page turns for 1 s with nothing marked, then the next page opens.
  • When a page opens it picks a point on the road within the mini's range (road sampled every 2 cm) and highlights 9 cm either side of it (scaled by the field keystones).
  • The pages cycle in order: the point furthest along the road (nearest the exit), the point nearest the entrance, the point nearest the atlas, then round again.
  • If no road is within reach, the page opens on nothing.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; it does not look at dudes to choose a page.

Effects

  • Every dude on the highlighted stretch (same route, distance along it inside the stretch) takes x1.3 from every hit while the page is open (+30%).
  • Two atlases do not stack: a dude on both stretches is answered by the lower-numbered atlas only.
  • It is a field for the aura keystones: InField is the open stretch, FieldTakes is x1.3, FieldRadius is 9 cm.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads no line stat: no damage, the page timing is fixed by 6 and 1, and the stretch is chosen within the mini's own range, not api.RangeOf, so range cards and auras do not widen it.
  • Deep Mark and Wide Field (SupportKeystoneRules.FieldScale) lengthen the stretch.

Quirks

  • The summary says a third more; the code gives +30%.
  • No sight check: the stretch is marked through walls and hills.
  • The first page opens 0.2 s after it is placed.
  • Different damage towers still stack with it: a Rules Lawyer or Seat-Belt Buckle on the same dude multiplies on top.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, DamageTaken, InField, FieldTakes, FieldReach
  • Engine verbs its code calls: none

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Bot placement: reach 24 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire it picks every living dude within the radius (plus half its scale, flat distance) of the target's feet.
  • Each of them not marked channel-bored is marked channel (4 s, or 3 s for a boss or non-grunt) and channel-bored for that time plus 4.5 s.
  • Every dude in the group, bored or not, is struck for the full card damage.

Targeting

  • No sight check for the group around the target; only the engine's target needs to be seen.

Effects

  • A dude marked channel takes x1.2 from every hit from any tower (DamageTaken).
  • channel-bored blocks a new channel mark until it wears off, so a dude is tuned in at most 4 s out of every 4 + 4.5 s.
  • channel is a decoy mark: the tower earns decoy time for it.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage is f.CardDamage; group radius is api.RadiusOf with a 3 cm floor (4.5 cm on the base card), so Radius cards widen the group.
  • Repeats are ignored.
  • Hits go through Strike with no direction: card on-hit effects apply, Force does nothing, shields never block.

Quirks

  • The summary says the group turns round and walks the wrong way and a boss only hesitates; the code turns nobody (issue 439). TurnSpeed (-0.8) and BossSpeed (0.25) are never used.
  • Several Remote Controls stack: the engine multiplies every tower's DamageTaken, so two give x1.44 on a dude marked by either.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire, DamageTaken
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, RadiusOf, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: channel, channel-bored
  • Marks it reads: channel, channel-bored
  • Named outside its folder in: Game/CcWall.cs

  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 60 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire it launches 10 kernels plus 3 per Repeat, each at a random point within 3.6 cm of where the target will be after that kernel's flight time.
  • Each kernel's flight time is random between 0.4 and 0.65 s, so the shower comes down over about a quarter of a second.
  • Kernels only land on terrain; where one lands it strikes every dude within 1.5 cm (plus half its scale, flat distance) for the full card damage, with no falloff.

Targeting

  • Lobs, so sight follows the cannon's arc rules.
  • The scatter point is weighted to the middle (random radius times 3.6), and it leads the target by its current speed.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Every kernel carries the full f.CardDamage; RepeatDamage is ignored, Repeats only add 3 kernels each.
  • The splash radius is the fixed 1.5 cm; the line's Radius stat is ignored.
  • Hits go through Strike with no direction, so card on-hit effects apply but Force does nothing (the base card's Force 1 is wasted) and shields never block.

Quirks

  • Kernels are random: one aimed at a lone dude can miss it entirely.
  • Two kernels landing close together can both hit the same dude.
  • The const Shove (0.7) is declared but never used; nothing it does shoves.
  • The Fire comment says nine kernels; the code throws 10.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, OnProjectileLand, CreateProjectile
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Launch, Strike

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Projectile (default Fire): a bolt at 120 cm/s, hit radius 1.35 cm round the chest, pierces with the Pierce stat (-15% a pass).
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 120 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Flicks a coin at 120 cm/s aimed ahead of the target, with a random jitter of up to 1.1 cm sideways and 0.5 cm up.
  • On a hit it does one Strike of the coin's card damage.
  • Then the coin ricochets once: it turns towards the nearest other living dude within 9 cm of the one it hit that terrain does not hide, and flies straight at it.
  • The ricochet hit takes 15% less (the engine's pierce loss) and does one more Strike; after that the coin is spent.

Targeting

  • The ricochet target is the nearest dude to the first one hit, not a boss or the engine's choice.

Effects

  • Kills by this line pay a bounty of x1.5 (rounded), from the card base's LootBonus of 0.5.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage is the projectile's card damage, through Strike, so on-hit cards apply to both hits.
  • Repeats give extra coins with a wider random spread (the default Fire).
  • Pierce above 1 lets the coin carry on in a straight line through more dudes after the ricochet, each pass 15% weaker; it never bounces twice.
  • LootBonus cards raise the kill bounty up to the 1.0 cap.

Quirks

  • The ricochet flies straight at where the second dude was, without homing, so it can miss a moving dude.
  • With nobody within 9 cm the coin stops at the first hit.
  • The summary comment says it is the only tower that pays for its own kills; the loot bonus is an ordinary stat that a card in the painters pack also gives.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: LaunchVelocity, OnProjectileHit, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Blocked, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Projectile (default Fire): lobbed shell, hit radius 0 cm round the chest.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 55 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 22 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Default lob: one cushion per shot (plus one per card Repeat, scattered) on a cannon arc, aimed by the engine at 55 cm/s.
  • When the cushion lands, every dude within the line's Radius (at least 2.5 cm, base 4) plus half its scale, measured flat, takes the full card damage through Strike as an area hit.

Effects

  • None. Nobody is slowed, held or shoved (issue 439). The landing is a damage burst only.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage, via the default Fire), Radius (through api.RadiusOf, minimum 2.5 cm), Range and FireRate through the engine, and Repeats (extra cushions).
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply. The hits have no direction, so the Force of 2 in its stat block gives no knockback.
  • Its splash is its own loop, not DamageAround: no falloff toward the edge, and a damage aura still applies.

Quirks

  • The class summary says it slows to a crawl and shoves back. The code does neither. SmotherSecs, SlowSecs and ShoveBack are declared but never used.
  • Its tip says 'SMOTHERS A PATCH', but there is no patch: the hit happens once, at landing.
  • A lob only lands on terrain (HitRadius 0), so a cushion never hits a dude in the air on the way down.
  • The CardReview list counts it as a pusher, although it pushes nothing.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnProjectileLand, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: RadiusOf, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

The Conker #

T-060 · conker · treehouse · 110 points

Home
