crowd control (its card pool)
Also called
Conker
Tray blurb
A conker on a string, swinging over the road. Time it and the horde bowls off to the side.
Tray tag line
SWINGS SIDEWAYS

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsMelee, Knockbackwhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit70.21 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.4 /severy 2.4 s (0.417 /s), the mini's own interval
Range8 cm10.5 cm, the mini's own range
Force33 cm shove for a grunt from a hit that travels (scaled by weight), if its code strikes with travel
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 8 cm.
  • Range mismatch: the card base says 8 cm but the mini's range is 10.5 cm. The game uses the mini's; the card number only scales it.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • It never fires: every frame the conker swings as a pendulum (2.6 s a full swing, up to 0.95 rad either side on a 4.1 cm string) from the tip of a 5.6 cm arm.
  • A living dude is hit when the ball is within 1.5 cm plus half the dude's size of it on the flat, and the ball is between 0.5 cm below its feet and 3.6 x its size above them.
  • A hit needs the ball to be moving at at least 30% of its top speed (so nothing is hit near the ends of the swing).
  • Each hit shoves the dude sideways across the road, staggers it, and makes one Strike of the line's card damage along the ball's flat velocity (power 0.6).
  • Each hit counts as a shot and plays the mini's fire animation.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: it never aims at a dude. Every 1.2 s it looks for the nearest point of any road within 14 cm (sampled every 2 cm) and turns its arm towards it.
  • The swing runs along the arm's direction, towards and away from the tower, so it crosses the road when the arm points straight at it.
  • It hits whatever the ball touches, on any route, with no sight check; flock patches do not hide dudes from it.
  • A dude marked conkered is skipped; the mark lasts 2.2 s after each hit.

Effects

  • Lane shove: 0.8 of a lane (lanes run -0.9 to 0.9 across the road), 0.4 x that for a big dude (boss or any non-grunt tier). The side follows the ball's sideways motion, or the dude's side of the tower when that motion is under 0.5 cm/s.
  • Stagger: 0.45 s, half that for a big dude.
  • Marks sprung for 1.5 s on every dude it hits.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • It reads only the line's Damage stat (passed straight to Strike); Range, FireRate, Repeats and Radius are never read, so cards that change them do nothing to it.
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects, the damage type and the line's Force knockback (along the ball's velocity) apply.
  • It never asks CanShoot, so the holdfire switch and cards that stop a tower firing do not stop the swing.

Quirks

  • Lane shoves go through Shove, which only clamps the lane offset; they are not part of the along-the-road shove budget.
  • Its sprung mark is the same key the Spring Sergeant uses, so the Sergeant will not toss a dude the conker hit in the last 1.5 s (it looks for another dude).
  • If the ball is moving faster than 300 cm/s on the flat (the god-hand carrying it), nothing is hit.
  • It hits nothing while held in the hand.
  • With no road within 14 cm the arm does not turn, but the ball still swings and hits any dude that walks under it.

Home table

  • After a hit, a dude that is still alive and stands within 1.2 cm of a plank gap along its road is sent down it (SendDown). Only tables with holes have gaps (the treehouse, its home table); big walkers never go down.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, SendDown, Shove, Stagger, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: conkered, sprung
  • Marks it reads: conkered
  • Named outside its folder in: Tables/Treehouse/TreehouseTable.cs

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Field: radius 20 cm (aura keystones read it).
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 16 cm.
  • Range mismatch: the card base says 20 cm but the mini's range is 22 cm. The game uses the mini's; the card number only scales it.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Runs itself in Update. It never shoots and does no damage.
  • Once a second it finds the road points (every 2 cm) within 0.8 x its reach. It aims the beam's centre at their average direction, and the beam is off when there are none.
  • It sweeps ±32 degrees round that direction, a full sweep every 5.5 s.
  • Every frame, every dude within 20 cm of the lens (flat, plus half its size) and inside the 28 degree cone of the beam's real heading gets lit.
  • Every 4.5 s it pulses (the first one 1.5 s after it is placed). On a pulse, the dudes in the beam that frame are lit for 2.5 s instead of 0.35 s.

Targeting

  • No target: it lights whoever stands in the cone.
  • No sight check: walls and terrain do not block the light.

Effects

  • It lays the mark torch-lit (0.35 s, refreshed every frame in the beam; 2.5 s on a pulse) and the plain mark lit for the same time.
  • While torch-lit is on a dude, every hit on it from a tower other than a Torch does +25% (DamageTaken).
  • Field keystones: InField is the torch-lit mark. Deep Mark and Wide Field scale the reach through SupportKeystoneRules.FieldScale.
  • 5 moths circle the lit dudes. This is decoration only.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads no stat from its line. Its reach, cone, sweep and pulse are constants, so Damage, Range, FireRate, Radius, range auras and rate auras change nothing. Only the field keystones scale the reach.

Quirks

  • The bonus stacks between Torches. Every placed Torch multiplies a lit dude's damage by 1.25 (the mark is shared), so two Torches give x1.5625.
  • The bonus applies to hits from any tower, splash hits included.
  • unclear from code: nothing in Assets/Greytide reads the plain lit mark (the Night Light and Stage Light lay it too).

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, DamageTaken, InField, FieldTakes
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked
  • Marks it puts on dudes: lit, torch-lit
  • Marks it reads: torch-lit

Card unitsIn play

Delivery tagsProjectile, Splashwhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit40.12 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.42 /severy 2.4 s (0.417 /s), the mini's own interval
Range20 cm20 cm, the mini's own range
Radius4.4 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Projectile (default Fire): lobbed shell, hit radius 0 cm round the chest.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 60 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 17 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Default lob: one balloon per shot (plus one per card Repeat).
  • On landing it does a DamageAround splash of radius 4.2 cm for the card damage, with the engine's falloff.
  • Then every dude within 4.2 cm (flat, plus half its size) is soaked and has its fire put out.
  • Then it lays a wet patch where it landed.

Effects

  • Soak: mark wet for 6 s, and a 1.2 s slow to 60% speed (90% for bosses and above-grunt enemies).
  • Douse: clears Ignite and any Fire damage over time on everyone it soaks, and every frame on everyone standing on a patch.
  • Wet patch: radius 4.2 cm, lasts 14 s, at most 4 per tower (the oldest goes). A balloon landing on an existing patch only renews it.
  • Standing on a patch: the dude keeps the wet mark (1.5 s, refreshed) and walks at 80% speed (bosses and above-grunt enemies 92%).
  • The first step onto a patch is a slip: a stagger of 0.5 s and a push back of 21 cm, both halved for big dudes. A slip happens at most once every 6 s per dude (mark slipped).
  • Through WetRule, its wet mark makes Cold and Shock towers do x1.5 while a Water Tower or Shower Head is placed. Every Watering Can in play adds +15% from all towers.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage), Repeats, and Range and FireRate through the engine.
  • The splash, soak and patch radius is the constant 4.2 cm. The line's Radius stat (base 4.4) is ignored.
  • The splash goes through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply. Being DamageAround, a damage aura does not boost it, and Force gives no knockback.
  • The slow, slip and patches are its own code and do not scale with cards.

Quirks

  • The class summary says a slip loses three centimetres. The code pushes 21 cm, through the engine's shove rules with their diminishing returns.
  • The summary says wet is only a mark that combos read. The Water Tower/Shower Head rule and the Watering Can's bonus read the same wet mark.
  • It puts out Ignite from friendly towers too (the Trebuchet stones, Fire cards).
  • The slipped mark is shared, so a dude that slipped on one balloon's patch does not slip on another's for 6 s.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, OnProjectileLand, SpeedFactor, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, Mark, Marked, Push, Slow, Stagger
  • Marks it puts on dudes: slipped, wet
  • Marks it reads: slipped

The Squirrel #

T-063 · squirrel · treehouse · 90 points

Squirrel
Tray blurb
Darts out, robs the nearest enemy of a crumb and runs home with it. They stop to argue; she pays a bonus.
Tray tag line
ROBS FOR POINTS

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsMelee, Staggerwhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit50.15 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.22 /severy 5 s (0.2 /s), the mini's own interval
Range14 cm14 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 12 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.22 shots/s but the mini fires every 5 s (0.2/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Self-driven: when its pause is over it sends a runner after the nearest dude within 14 cm (flat) of the tower that does not carry robbed.
  • The runner runs at 42 cm/s to the dude and robs it on arrival.
  • Rob: mark robbed for 12 s, a hold, and one Strike of the line's card damage (base 5) with no direction.
  • The runner then stands 0.35 s and runs home.
  • Home with the crumb it pays 1 point, or 4 points on every 4th trip begun, then pauses 2.6 s.
  • The first trip can start 1.5 s after placing.

Targeting

  • Nearest dude by flat distance within 14 cm, no line of sight, no boss or hunted priority, skipping dudes with robbed.
  • Once out, the runner chases its dude wherever it goes; there is no range check during the chase.

Effects

  • Hold (Hold) for 1.4 s, or 1.4 x 0.4 s for a boss or mini-boss.
  • Mark robbed for 12 s: the same dude is not robbed again meanwhile.
  • Pays points through api.Pay (1, or 4 on every 4th trip).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads only the line's Damage (line.Stats[Stat.Damage]); the nip goes through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores Range (reach is the constant 14 cm) and FireRate (the pause is the constant 2.6 s), so range and rate cards and auras change nothing.
  • Ignores Repeats, Radius and Force.

Quirks

  • It does not check CanShoot.
  • A runner whose dude dies before it arrives comes back empty and pays nothing; that trip still counts towards the shiny count.
  • Picked up mid-trip, the runner vanishes and the trip pays nothing.
  • The summary says a trip every 5 s or so; the code waits 2.6 s after each return plus the run, and does not use the mini's 5 s interval.
  • The robbed mark is not one of the engine's decoy marks, so it earns no decoy time.
  • The hold is the engine's glue hold: a boss's or mini-boss's grip breaks after about 0.27 s, shorter than 1.4 x 0.4 s.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Hold, Mark, Marked, Pay, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: robbed
  • Marks it reads: robbed

Combos (Lucky)

SWEEPS BACK, SOAKS

Card unitsIn play

Radius5 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Bot placement: reach 14 cm.
  • Range mismatch: the card base says 19 cm but the mini's range is 20 cm. The game uses the mini's; the card number only scales it.
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 14 cm; its mini reaches 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each shot tips water down the target's road from 5 cm behind it to 4 cm in front (plus 0.5 cm at each end).
  • Every dude on that route in the stretch takes the card damage once through Strike as area damage.

Targeting

  • Only dudes on the same route as the target are touched, whatever their lane offset.

Effects

  • Each survivor is marked wet for 6 s.
  • A burning survivor is doused (its ignite is put out).
  • Each survivor is pushed back with api.Push(42); a boss or mini-boss gets 0.35 of that. The spec comment says 42 push units slide about 6 cm.
  • A grunt is slowed to 65% speed for 3 s; bosses and mini-bosses are not slowed.
  • While a dude carries wet, every hit on it from any tower is multiplied by 1.15 (+15%).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Damage is f.CardDamage via Strike, so on-hit card effects apply.
  • The stretch length is fixed by 5 and 4; the line's Radius stat is not read. Repeats and Force are not read by its code.

Quirks

  • The wet bonus answers for any wet mark, also those laid by the Watering Can, Water Balloon, Shower Head, Water Tower, Pint Giant and the Wet affinity combo rule.
  • It does not go through WetRule, so it stacks on top of the Cold/Shock x1.5 and multiplies once per Bucket in play.
  • The summary says only bosses are pushed less and never slowed; the code treats mini-bosses the same (IsBig).

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, DamageTaken
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Mark, Marked, Push, Slow, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: wet
  • Marks it reads: wet
  • Named outside its folder in: Tables/Castle/CastleTown.cs

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 14 cm.
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 14 cm; its mini reaches 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On each shot it flings sand in a cone aimed at the engine's target, half-angle 38 degrees (76 degrees across), out to its range.
  • Every visible dude in the cone takes one Strike of the card damage (base 10) through DamageCone, travelling along the aim, with no falloff.
  • Then the target alone: a boss or mini-boss is slowed; anyone else is buried 0.12 s later.

Targeting

  • The cone points at the engine's target, not the mini's facing.
  • The dude it buries is the engine's target (hunted, then boss, then nearest), not necessarily the nearest one.

Effects

  • Buried: Hold for 1.6 s and the mark buried for 1.6 s, with a sand heap drawn on it.
  • A boss or mini-boss target is slowed to 65% speed for 1.8 s instead.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads f.CardDamage and Range (api.RangeOf) for the cone's reach.
  • Cone hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores Repeats and the line's Radius (base 5); the cone angle is fixed.

Quirks

  • No code reads the buried mark.
  • When the target is a boss or mini-boss nobody is buried that shot.
  • The hold is the engine's glue hold: a grunt held 1.8 s in total from any source breaks free and cannot be held for 4 s, and a held grunt still moves at the 45% slow floor.
  • One heap per spade: a new burial moves the heap to the new dude.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, DamageCone, Hold, Mark, RangeOf, Slow
  • Marks it puts on dudes: buried

T-066 · ant-nest · sandpit · 90 points

Tray blurb
Sends a line of ants down the road to meet the horde. Whoever walks through is marked and starts to itch.
Tray tag line
MARKS AND ITCHES

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsPulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit20.06 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.28 /severy 3.5 s (0.286 /s), the mini's own interval
Range17 cm17 cm, the mini's own range
Radius3 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at instant speed.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Bot placement: reach 14 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • A shot sends a line of 14 ants from the road point nearest the nest (searched within 24 cm of the target along its route).
  • The line marches at 20 cm/s towards the target: forward if the target has passed that point, back if it is still coming. It stops 2 cm past the target's position, at most 26 cm from the start.
  • The line is 8 cm long and lasts 5.5 s from the shot, march included.
  • Every 0.6 s it nibbles each dude standing in the line for half the line's Damage stat, through Strike as area damage (no direction).

Targeting

  • Only one line per tower: a shot while the current line is younger than 5.5 s minus 0.6 s does nothing, so with the mini's 3.5 s reload a new line goes out about every 7 s.
  • It only touches dudes on the same route as the line, between its head and tail (plus 0.7 cm), whatever their lane offset.

Effects

  • A dude in the line is marked ants for 4 s, refreshed every frame it stays in.
  • While a dude carries ants, every hit on it from any tower is multiplied by 1.25 (+25%), the nest's own nibbles included.
  • A dude in the line itches at a chance of 1 per second: a stagger of 0.32 s (half that for a boss or mini-boss), then mark itch-rest for 2 s, during which it cannot itch.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • The nibble reads line.Stats[Damage] directly (x 0.5), not f.CardDamage; it goes through Strike, so on-hit card effects apply.
  • Radius, Range (beyond the mini's targeting), Force, Repeats and Pierce are not read by its code.

Quirks

  • The damage bonus is asked of every Ant Nest in play, and the engine multiplies them: with two nests a dude marked ants takes 1.25 x 1.25 from every hit.
  • The ants keep nibbling, marking and itching while the tower is held from firing.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, Ready, Fire, DamageTaken
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked, Stagger, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: ants, itch-rest
  • Marks it reads: ants, itch-rest

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never shoots. Self-driven clock while placed: the flag flutters for the first 3 s of every 8 s and hangs limp for the rest, starting with a gust.

Effects

  • Aura while fluttering: every tower on the eight tiles round it gets +40% damage, +20% rate and +15% range; nothing while limp.
  • Treasure: every kill by a placed tower on those eight tiles pays this flag's table 2 Points while fluttering or 1 while limp, on top of the bounty.
  • Several flags round one tower each pay for its kill and each give their aura.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads no line stat: no damage, and the cycle is fixed by 8 and 3.
  • Its own cards change nothing it does.

Quirks

  • The summary says limp first, then a gust; the clock starts with the gust.
  • The clock only runs while placed, so lifting it pauses the cycle where it is.
  • The treasure pays for any kill by a neighbour, whatever killed and wherever the dude was.

Checks tickets. Fare-dodgers are shown onto the line just before the train comes through. All change!

ONTO THE LINE

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play

Damage per hit50.15 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.333 /severy 5 s (0.2 /s), the mini's own interval
Range14 cm14 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 14 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.333 shots/s but the mini fires every 5 s (0.2/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Ticket check: when his reload is up and at least one dude is within 14 cm (flat) that is not marked ticket-checked and not being moved, he checks them all at once; the reload is then 3 s.
  • Each checked dude is held (0.35 s for a grunt, 0.2 s for a mini-boss, 0.12 s for a boss), marked ticket-checked for 6 s and takes one Strike of the line's card damage with no push.
  • His first check can come 1.5 s after he is placed; with nobody new in reach he checks again on the first frame someone is.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: everyone within 14 cm flat distance, no sight check, skipping dudes checked in the last 6 s.

Effects

  • The hold goes through api.Hold, so the glue rules apply (bosses resist, the lane is never held for ever).
  • ticket-checked and fare-checked are his marks; neither is a decoy mark.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Only the line's Damage stat is read (through Strike, so on-hit cards apply).
  • Range and FireRate on his line are ignored: reach is the constant 14 cm and the check interval the constant 3 s.

Quirks

  • He never checks CanShoot, so he keeps checking tickets during hold-fire.
  • Bosses and mini-bosses are only ticket-checked, never moved onto the line.

Home table

  • Whenever a railway line is active (the railway table), each dude in his reach that the next train's engine will reach in 0.8 to 7 s gets one fare check for that train (mark fare-checked).
  • A movable grunt-tier dude is a fare-dodger with chance 80%; movable means not a boss or mini-boss, not carried, immune, raised, lifted or walking backwards, within 16 cm of the rails, away from the tunnel portals and not across the river gorge.
  • A fare-dodger stands still where it is (no patience earned) until the engine is 1.5 s away; then he whistles 'ALL CHANGE!' (once per train per conductor) and it hops onto the ballast in 0.4 s.
  • It waits on the ballast until the train's tail has passed, the train is gone, or 4 s; then it hops back to its road in 0.5 s. Its route position is never changed.
  • The train does the damage; a dude he moved that the train kills counts as his kill (his Kills and the balance stats).
  • All conductors share one list of moved dudes, so two never take the same one; removing him puts his dudes back on the road at once.
  • A fare-dodger can stand still for up to about 7 s in the queue plus 4 s on the ballast; this stop is not an api.Hold, so the hold rules and caps do not limit it.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Hold, Mark, Marked, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: fare-checked, ticket-checked
  • Marks it reads: fare-checked, ticket-checked

LIVE WITH A TRAIN

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 30 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 1 shots/s but the mini fires every 0.8 s (1.25/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 30 cm; its mini reaches 16 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Runs itself in Update and never uses the engine's aim or fire.
  • When its cooldown is up, it gathers every living dude within 16 cm (flat distance from its base). If there are none, it waits and checks again the next frame.
  • If anyone is in reach it shocks them all at once. Each takes the line's card damage x1, times 2.2 while a train is running, through Strike as an area hit (no direction).
  • After a shock the cooldown is 1 s. The first shock can come 0.5 s after it is placed.

Targeting

  • No target picking: it hits every dude in the radius, on the track or off it.
  • No sight check: walls, hills and flock patches do not protect anyone.

Effects

  • Shock damage type, so the engine applies the Shock statuses from its line.
  • Live rail: while RailwayLine.Active has a running train, the shock is x2.2 and the mini glows steadily.
  • The wet mark: Wet dudes take x1.5 from it through the Water Tower or Shower Head rule (WetRule) while one of those is placed, because its base damage type is Shock.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads only line.Stats[Stat.Damage] (card damage). The reach (16 cm) and the interval (1 s) are constants, so Range, Radius, FireRate, Repeats, range auras and rate auras do not change it.
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects, DoT share and statuses apply. The hit has no direction, so Force gives no knockback.
  • Its own loop is not a DamageAround splash, so a damage aura (Captain Lightbox) does boost it, and there is no falloff.

Quirks

  • It does not check api.CanShoot, so the holdfire switch and cards that stop a tower shooting do not stop it.
  • It is flagged Supports, so the tower check only asks it to damage a grunt.
  • unclear from code: the summary says it cannot stand on the track hexes. Nothing in its folder enforces this, so it depends on the board.

Home table

  • It can only go live on the railway table: RailwayLine.Active exists only there. On any other table it always shocks at the base strength.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Strike

Combos (Bright)

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play

Damage per hit0none
Fire rate0.213 /severy 4 s (0.25 /s), the mini's own interval
Range12 cm12 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Modifies damage: its DamageTaken hook multiplies every tower's hits (see its note).
  • Field: radius 12 cm (aura keystones read it).
  • Bot placement: reach 9.6 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.213 shots/s but the mini fires every 4 s (0.25/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Runs itself in Update. It never shoots and does no damage.
  • The cycle is cooldown, then armed, then pour. The first cooldown is 0.3 x 2.5 s; after that it is 2.5 s.
  • Armed: it waits until any living dude is within 12 cm (flat) of it.
  • Pour: it lasts 2.2 s. Every 0.25 s during it, every dude within 12 cm is marked wet for 4 s.

Targeting

  • No target: it affects everyone in the radius.
  • No sight check.

Effects

  • Mark wet (4 s).
  • DamageTaken (WetRule): a wet dude takes x1.5 from any tower whose base damage type is Cold or Shock.
  • Only the first placed soaker in the slot list (Water Tower or Shower Head) answers the rule, so any number of soakers give x1.5 at most.
  • Field keystones: the field is its reach. InField means wet and within reach, and Deep Mark and Wide Field scale the reach.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads no stat from its line. The reach and timings are constants, so cards, range auras and rate auras change nothing, except the field keystones scaling the reach.
  • The bonus checks the attacker's base damage type, not the type of the line or card that hits.

Quirks

  • Its x1.5 applies to wet marks from any source, including a Water Balloon, a Watering Can or the combo engine, as long as it is placed.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update, DamageTaken, InField, FieldTakes
  • Engine verbs its code calls: Mark, Marked
  • Marks it puts on dudes: wet
  • Marks it reads: wet

STOPS, CHILLS, NIPS

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typeColdno status of its own (the Chill tag chills, not the type)
Delivery tagsPulsewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit30.09 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.115 /severy 8 s (0.125 /s), the mini's own interval
Range8.8 cm8.8 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 7.04 cm.
  • Rate mismatch: the card base says 0.115 shots/s but the mini fires every 8 s (0.125/s). The game uses the mini's interval; the card number only scales it (a card that adds 20% Fire Rate makes the mini 20% faster).

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • It never fires; its own Update runs a cycle of cooling, armed, amber warning and danger.
  • The first cooldown after placing is 6 x 0.4 s; later cooldowns are 6 s.
  • When the cooldown ends it waits (armed) until any living dude stands within 8.8 cm (flat distance) of the tower.
  • Then the lamp goes amber for 0.7 s, and the signal drops to danger for 2 s.
  • Every 0.5 s while at danger, every dude within 8.8 cm takes a Strike of the line's card damage (base 3), with no direction and no shove.
  • Every frame while at danger, every dude within 8.8 cm is held for the rest of the danger time plus 0.05 s, and chilled.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: no aim, no target; it acts on every dude within 8.8 cm flat of its base, with no line-of-sight check.
  • A dude that walks into reach while it is at danger is held for what is left of the danger.

Effects

  • Hold (Hold) for the rest of the danger, up to 2 s.
  • A boss or mini-boss is held only until 0.8 s after the drop to danger.
  • On the first frame a dude is caught: one Chill stack (cap 2) and a slow to 55% speed for 4.5 s (boss or mini-boss: 80% for 2 s).
  • On every later frame while at danger the slow is topped up (same speed and seconds), so the slow runs from the end of the danger.
  • Its line is Cold, so the ticks carry the engine's Cold statuses and the Wet x1.5.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads only the line's Damage (line.Stats[Stat.Damage]) for the tick; the ticks go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores the line's Range: reach is the constant 8.8 cm, so range cards and range auras change nothing.
  • Ignores FireRate: the cycle is fixed (6 + 0.7 + 2 s at the shortest), so rate cards and auras change nothing.
  • Ignores Repeats, Radius and Force.

Quirks

  • It does not check CanShoot, so the holdfire switch and cards that stop a tower shooting do not stop it.
  • The engine's glue grip limits every hold: a grunt is released after 1.8 s held in total (less than the 2 s danger) and then cannot be held for 4 s.
  • Under the glue grip a boss or mini-boss shrugs off 85% of a hold, so it breaks free after about 0.27 s, sooner than 0.8 s.
  • A held dude is not stopped dead: the engine's slow floor keeps a grunt at 45% speed or more, a boss or mini-boss at 70%.
  • The cycle only advances while it is placed; picked up, its phase and timer pause where they are.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Hold, Slow, Strike

CAN'T HIT WITHIN 18 CM

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play

Damage per hit362.88 hp (x DamageScale 0.222 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.222 /severy 4.5 s (0.222 /s), the mini's own interval
Range55 cm55 cm, the mini's own range; dead zone 18 cm
Radius6 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: range only: walls, houses, hills and table blockers never hide a dude from it
  • Prefers far: a boss or mini-boss in range first, then the farthest dude it can see.
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity so high nothing blocks it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 45 cm/s.
  • A hit ends the projectile whatever its Pierce.
  • Bot placement: reach 45 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • One stone per shot is launched at 60 degrees. Its flight time comes from flat distance, clamped to 0.35 to 1.6 s.
  • Its aim is led twice on the target's walk, then pushed out to the edge of the dead zone if it falls inside.
  • Each card Repeat adds a stone with random scatter for the line's repeat damage.
  • A stone bursts when it touches a dude (HitRadius 1.1, it ends there) or the ground.
  • The burst is a DamageAround splash of radius max(3, the line's Radius; base 6 cm) for the stone's card damage, with the engine's falloff.

Targeting

  • PrefersFar: hunted first, then a boss or any above-grunt enemy in reach, then the farthest it can see.
  • OverWalls: only range matters for sight. Walls, houses, hills and the castle's gatehouse hide nobody from it.
  • Dead zone (SkipTarget): it never takes a dude closer than 18 cm (flat) to its base. It also skips one whose led position after the stone's flight would be inside the zone, and it drops a target that walks in. Card shots obey this too.

Effects

  • Fire damage type: through Strike the line's Fire statuses apply, which is how its dudes are Ignited.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage), Repeats and RepeatDamage, Radius (minimum 3 cm), and Range (through the engine, and api.RangeOf for the crew check). The dead zone is the constant 18 cm.
  • Its hits go through Strike (inside DamageAround), so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Being a splash, a damage aura (Captain Lightbox) does not boost it, and a rate aura gives it only half.
  • Force gives no knockback, since splash hits have no direction.

Quirks

  • Its default LaunchVelocity is not used: Fire aims every stone itself.
  • The tip text hard-codes 'CAN'T HIT WITHIN 18 CM'. It does not follow DeadZone if that changes.
  • The Blurb says 'the biggest dude in reach'. The code takes any boss or above-grunt enemy first, then the farthest, not the largest.
  • A Water Balloon soak puts out the Ignite it leaves.

Home table

  • Castle counter-battery: on its home castle table, while the ladder crew carries the ladder (CastleSession.CrewAt), each shot aims at the crew instead of its target. This holds as long as the crew is at least 18 cm away and within 1.1x its range.
  • It still needs an ordinary target in reach to fire at all.
  • A burst within 4 cm (flat) of the crew calls DelayBreach: the breach comes 6 s later, the crew walks back, and it shows 'SIEGE BROKEN'.
  • This works once per game for all Trebuchets together, because the counter lives in one shared log.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, OnProjectileHit, OnProjectileLand, SkipTarget, CreateProjectile
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, Launch, OnHomeTable, RangeOf
  • Shared rules it reads: Balance.Accuracy
  • Named outside its folder in: Tables/Castle/CastleTowerCheck.cs

3 ARROWS, 3 DUDES

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsProjectilewhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit60.18 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.8 /severy 1.25 s (0.8 /s), the mini's own interval
Range26 cm26 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 150 cm/s.
  • A hit ends the projectile whatever its Pierce.
  • Bot placement: reach 24 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each shot is a volley of 3 arrows at 150 cm/s, loosed 0.08 s apart from the archer's hand.
  • Each arrow is aimed with lead at its own dude and does the full card damage.
  • An arrow hits the first dude it meets (HitRadius 1.0) and stops there. The hit goes through Strike from the arrow's direction.
  • Each hit is multiplied by the height bonus: +4% per cm the tower's base stands above the dude's feet, up to +30%, nothing for a dude above it.

Targeting

  • Arrow 1 goes to the engine's target.
  • Arrows 2 and 3 go to the next nearest living dudes (3D distance from the eye) within its range, adjusted for height, and in line of sight. They do not consider hunted dudes or bosses.
  • With fewer than three distinct dudes, the spare arrows go round the picked ones again.
  • A delayed arrow whose dude has died is not loosed at all.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads Damage (f.CardDamage, the same for all three arrows) and Range (api.RangeOf, for the extra picks).
  • Repeats are ignored: the volley is always three. Pierce is ignored, because every hit ends the arrow.
  • Hits go through Strike with a direction, so card on-hit effects and Force apply (base Force 0).

Quirks

  • Arrows fly straight and can be caught by a different dude in the way.
  • The pop text '+25%' is hard-coded and does not follow OutsideBonus.

Home table

  • On its home castle table, an arrow that hits a dude outside the wall (CastleSession.Outside: beyond the wall's outer face, the ladder's climbers included) does x1.25.
  • Those hits get an orange flash. A '+25%' pop shows at most every 4 s for all Lego-lases together.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, OnProjectileHit, OnProjectileLand, CreateProjectile
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Blocked, Launch, OnHomeTable, RangeOf, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Tables/Castle/CastleTowerCheck.cs

Sir Brickalot #

T-079 · sir-brickalot · castle · 140 points

Home
The Castle Siege (in that table's tray)
Number
T-079, TowerKind 57
Price
140 points
Affinity
Lucky
Role
shooter (its card pool)
Also called
Brickalot, brickalot, knight
Tray blurb
Rides out, lances the nearest dude and trots home; nothing hides a dude from him. HE HITS THE CASTLE OGRE 50% HARDER.
Tray tag line
RIDES OUT TO THEM

Base attack (from the card base and the mini)

Card unitsIn play
Damage typePhysicalno status of its own
Delivery tagsMelee, Staggerwhat card filters see; the tower's code decides the real shape
Damage per hit1444.32 hp (x DamageScale 1/12 x Balance.Damage 0.36)
Fire rate0.5 /severy 2 s (0.5 /s), the mini's own interval
Range24 cm24 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Carries through shields: what a shield can't hold goes on to the dude.
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Self-driven: at his post, once his rest is over and CanShoot allows, he picks a dude and rides out at 30 cm/s.
  • He rides in a straight line from his post to the point of the dude's road nearest the post, then along the road to the dude.
  • Within 1.6 cm (along the road) of the dude he lances it: one Strike of the line's card damage (base 144) and a Stagger of 0.8 s.
  • After the lance he stands 0.35 s, then rides back the same way.
  • Back at the post he rests 1 / FireRate s (2 s at base) before the next sortie; the rest runs faster with a rate aura.
  • He turns back without striking if the dude dies, or gets more than 1.4 x his reach along the road from his join point.

Targeting

  • Picks the dude cheapest to reach: hop from post to road plus distance along the road, which must be less than his reach (RangeOf, base 24 cm).
  • Only roads whose nearest point is within 12 cm of his post count; dudes on other roads are ignored.
  • No line-of-sight check: houses, walls and the castle gatehouse do not hide dudes from him.
  • No boss priority and no hunted priority: only the cheapest ride counts.
  • Ignores dudes that are not on a route (no valid road index).

Effects

  • Stagger for 0.8 s on every lance (api.Stagger), besides whatever the Stagger delivery tag gives through Strike.
  • Against the castle's Ogre (EnemyKind.CastleOgre) the lance does 1.5 x the card damage.
  • His hits carry through shields (CarriesThroughShield): when a lance breaks a dude's front shield, the damage the shield could not hold goes on into the dude.
  • The lance travels from him to the dude, so it can meet a front shield when he rides at a dude walking towards him.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the line's Damage (line.Stats[Stat.Damage]), Range (api.RangeOf) and FireRate (the rest at the post).
  • Lances go through Strike (power 3), so card on-hit effects apply.
  • Ignores Repeats, Radius, Force and Pierce.

Quirks

  • The mini itself moves: it rides on the road and the tower's root follows him.
  • The Ogre has 125 base hp and 0.5 flat armour; a base lance on him is 144 x 1.5 = 216 card damage before the toy scale.
  • The Ogre stops at the gate for 5 blows, 1.3 s apart; he is a standing target there and the gatehouse does not hide him from the knight.
  • The BuildBase comment says a 144 lance is 12 hp; with DamageScale 1/12 and the game's x 0.36 it is about 4.3 hp.

Home table

  • Castle perk 'To the breach!': fires once per game, the first frame the castle wall is breached, if a knight saw the wall whole earlier.
  • Every knight on the castle that is not already in a breach fight, within 1 s of the perk firing, rides home first if he was out, then rides to the breach.
  • He takes the breach road (roads 4 to 7) whose nearest point is closest to his post, with no 12 cm limit, and rides to its point nearest the foot of the inner stairs.
  • There he fights for 10 s: every 0.8 s (rest shortened by a rate aura) he lances the nearest dude within 6 cm flat, on any road, with the same lance and Stagger.
  • The breach lances do not check CanShoot.
  • Then he rides home and goes back to normal sorties; a knight placed more than 1 s after the breach never goes.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: CanShoot, OnHomeTable, RangeOf, Stagger, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Tables/Castle/CastleTowerCheck.cs

POURS ON ITS ROAD

Radius2.6 cmsplash or pulse radius, if its code reads it
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it; its Update hook runs it.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • No coverage goal: it stands on the road or only buffs, so the table check does not ask it to reach the road.
  • Bot placement: reach 8 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • It pours on a fixed spot: the nearest road point (sampled every 0.25 cm) within its range, flat distance from its cell's centre; with no road in range it never pours.
  • When its reload is up, it may shoot, and a dude is within 2.6 + 2.5 cm of the spot (and within 3 cm of its height), it starts a pour; the oil lands 0.35 s later.
  • The landing does one Strike of the line's card damage (taken when the pour starts) on every dude within 2.6 cm plus 0.35 x its scale of the spot and within 3 cm of its height.
  • It leaves a burning patch for 3 s that hits everyone in it for 30% of the pour's card damage every 0.5 s.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: it does not aim at a dude, so walls, houses and the gatehouse never block it; it only waits for someone near its spot.

Effects

  • Fire damage: each hit can Ignite through the engine's statuses.
  • Each patch is noted as a control zone for its lifetime.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reach is api.RangeOf; reload is api.IntervalOf (cards and auras included).
  • Damage is the line's Damage stat, through Strike for the pour and every patch tick, so on-hit cards apply and a Captain Lightbox's damage bonus applies too.
  • The Radius stat is not read: the splash and patch are always 2.6 cm.
  • Repeats and Pierce are ignored.
  • Its delivery is Splash, so a Captain Lightbox gives it half the fire-rate bonus.

Quirks

  • It pours on its spot, not on a dude, so a fast dude can be past before the oil lands.
  • Its patches go out at once when the tower is lifted or sold.
  • Dudes on a wall above the spot (more than 3 cm higher) are never hit.

Home table

  • Murder hole: on the castle, on a cell beside the gate passage, it pours only on road inside the gate passage within its range, and the patch there lasts 1.5 x 3 s; it pops 'MURDER HOLE' at most every 4 s.
  • With the perk on but no gate road within range it never pours, because all other road is excluded.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: OnSlotRemoved, Update
  • Engine verbs its code calls: CanShoot, IntervalOf, NoteZone, OnHomeTable, RangeOf, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Tables/Castle/CastleTowerCheck.cs

Range38 cm38 cm, the mini's own range
  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers; it hits along a line, so flock would hide dudes from it (no tower lays flock in play today)
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 190 cm/s.
  • A hit ends the projectile whatever its Pierce.
  • Bot placement: reach 30 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Each fire throws a burst of three darts at the target: the first at once, the second 0.17 s later and the third 2 x 0.17 s later, from the mini's hand.
  • Each dart flies straight at 190 cm/s, aimed at where the target will be (Ballistics.Lead), carrying the shot's card damage.
  • A dart hit is one Strike of its card damage along its flight; a dart that hits stops there.
  • If the first two darts both hit the aimed dude, the third dart hits that dude for 3 x card damage (a harder knock).

Targeting

  • The second and third darts are not thrown if the aimed dude has died or the tower has been lifted.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Reads the shot's card damage (Damage stat) for every dart; Range comes from the engine.
  • Its Fire ignores Repeats and RepeatDamage: a repeat card adds no darts.
  • Hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply; it ends a dart on its first hit, so Pierce does nothing.

Quirks

  • Retired: it belongs to the pub table, which is retired, so it is never offered in the tower wall or listed in the catalog.
  • A dart that hits a different dude than the aimed one hits at plain damage and does not count towards the third dart.
  • The streak is one per tower: a second fire call in the same volley (Second Barrel, Capacitor) starts another burst and resets the streak, so the first burst's third dart usually loses its x3.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Fire, OnProjectileHit, OnProjectileLand, CreateProjectile
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Launch, Strike
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Bot placement: reach 8 cm.
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 8 cm; its mini reaches 13.5 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire it waits 0.26 s, then slams; nothing happens if it was lifted or sold in that time.
  • The slam hits every living dude whose flat distance from the Giant's feet is between 2.4 cm (minus 0.4 x its size) and the outer radius (plus 0.4 x its size).
  • The outer radius is the line's Radius stat, at least 5 cm (12.5 cm on the base card).
  • Each dude in the ring is marked wet for 5 s and struck once for the full card damage, the hit travelling outward from the Giant.
  • The ring is then left on the table as a band for 5 s; twice a second every dude in the band (flat distance from about 0.77 x 2.4 cm out to the outer radius) is struck for 0.08 x the line's card damage.

Targeting

  • The engine's target only triggers the slam; what is hit is whoever stands in the ring round the Giant, with no sight check.
  • Dudes closer than about 2.4 cm to its feet are inside the hole of the ring and are not hit by the slam.

Effects

  • Marks every dude in the slam ring wet for 5 s; while a Shower Head or Water Tower is placed, that mark makes Cold and Shock hits on it count x1.5 (WetRule).
  • The wet band left behind is a damage zone (0.08 x card damage every 0.5 s), not a slow.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Slam damage is f.CardDamage; the band tick reads the line's Damage stat directly (x0.08).
  • Ring size reads the line's Radius stat (floor 5 cm); the Range stat only matters for the engine's targeting.
  • Repeats are ignored: one slam per fire.
  • All hits go through Strike, so card on-hit effects apply; the slam travels outward, so Force from cards knocks back (the base Force is 0), while the band ticks have no direction (no knockback, shields never block them).

Quirks

  • Retired: it belongs to the pub table, which is retired (PubTable.Retired), so it is not offered in play.
  • The summary says the slam shoves dudes back and sideways and the band slows them; the code does neither. ShoveCm (5), WetSlow (0.55), ShoveCooldown (2.6) and the pint-shoved mark are declared but never used.
  • The BuildBase comment says 28 card damage and force 3; the stat block is 30 damage and force 0.
  • The band tick clock is shared by all Giants (one 0.5 s timer for every ring), and a band vanishes early if its Giant is removed.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After, Mark, Marked, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: wet
  • Marks it reads: pint-shoved
  • Named outside its folder in: Cards/Packs/CardReview.cs, Game/CcWall.cs

  • Sight: a straight line from its eye to the dude's chest, clear of scenery and table blockers
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 95 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 14 cm.
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 14 cm; its mini reaches 20 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • On fire every living dude within its range (flat distance from the tower) that is not marked quizzed is marked quizzed for 4 s and struck for 0.5 x the card damage.
  • Of those, the dude furthest along its road that is a grunt-tier non-boss gets a question mark; if there is none, the furthest of any kind does.
  • After 1 s, if it is still alive, the question-mark dude is struck for 5 x the card damage.

Targeting

  • The engine's target is always asked, even when it stands outside the range circle.
  • Dudes already marked quizzed are skipped completely: no hit, and they cannot get the question mark.
  • No sight check: walls and hills do not stop the question.

Effects

  • Marks quizzed for 4 s (shared by every Quiz Master).

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Both hits scale with f.CardDamage; range is api.RangeOf.
  • Radius and Repeats are ignored.
  • All hits go through Strike: the crowd hit has no direction (no knockback, shields never block); the pop travels straight up.

Quirks

  • Retired: it belongs to the pub table, which is retired (PubTable.Retired), so it is not offered in play.
  • The summary says the crowd stops to think and the popped dude is staggered; the code holds and staggers nobody. ThinkSecs (1.2) and Stagger (1.2) are not used for any effect.
  • Bosses and mini-bosses get the question mark only when no grunt was asked, and then take the full pop.
  • If every dude in range is still quizzed (another Quiz Master asked them), nobody is hit and there is no pop.

  • Marks it reads: quizzed

  • Sight: lobbed: needs the cannon's arc clear from its eye to the dude's chest
  • Lobs: an arc under gravity.
  • Aims ahead for a shot at 60 cm/s.
  • Support: holds, slows or marks rather than kills (the tower check only asks that it damages a grunt).
  • Bot placement: reach 16 cm.
  • Bot reach: the bot places it as if it reached 16 cm; its mini reaches 22 cm.

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Lobs a pinch of crumbs at the point on the target's own route 6 cm ahead of it (clamped to the route's end), with a flight time of distance / 45 clamped to 0.45-0.95 s.
  • Where the crumbs land, a DamageAround of the shot's card damage over 2.6 cm.
  • Then it lays a crumb patch of radius 2.6 cm with 3 to 5 mouthfuls, plus one more 20% of the time (3 to 6). The patch lasts 8 s or until its mouthfuls run out.
  • Each frame, every small dude within 2.6 cm of a patch takes a mouthful: one Strike of 5 x the shot's card damage, with no travel direction (power 0.4).

Effects

  • After a mouthful the dude is marked crisp-full for 5 s and cannot take another from any packet's patch.
  • Small means: not a boss, and a grunt tier with under 6 hit points and a scale under 1.3 (a dude with no enemy definition counts as small). Everything else walks over the crumbs untouched.
  • At most 12 patches exist across all Crisp Packets; a 13th removes the oldest.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • The landing splash and the bites use the shot's card damage (Damage stat); bites are 5 x it.
  • The patch radius is the constant 2.6; the line's Radius stat is never read. Repeats are ignored (one pinch per shot).
  • Both the landing splash and the bites go through Strike (the splash via DamageAround), so card on-hit effects apply.

Quirks

  • Retired: it belongs to the pub table, which is retired, so it is never offered in the tower wall or listed in the catalog.
  • The summary and Blurb say small dudes stop to eat; the code never holds or slows them. A bite is only damage (issue 439 comment), and the 1.1 s eating timer only tracks who is mid-mouthful.
  • All Crisp Packets share one set of patches, ticked once per frame by whichever packet updates first; patches are only ticked while at least one packet is in the game.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, Fire, OnProjectileLand, CreateProjectile, PlayFireSound
  • Engine verbs its code calls: DamageAround, Launch, Mark, Marked, Strike
  • Marks it puts on dudes: crisp-full
  • Marks it reads: crisp-full
  • Named outside its folder in: Game/CcWall.cs

What its code does (read from code)

Attack

  • Never shoots. It builds pressure from 0 to 1 over 10 s while at least one neighbour (not another tender) has a living target.
  • At full pressure it vents: pressure drops to 0 and nothing is given for 1.6 s, then it builds again from 0.
  • With no neighbour firing it leaks pressure at 0.25 x the build speed. A new tender starts at 0.2.

Targeting

  • Self-driven: the engine never aims or fires it.

Effects

  • Aura: each tower on the four tiles beside it gets fire rate + pressure x 0.5 (up to +50%); a splash tower gets half of that.

How it uses its attack line (what cards can change)

  • Its code reads no line stats; the build and vent times are consts.

Quirks

  • Retired and dev-only: DevOnly is true (Joe, 2026-10-20: the Water Tower replaces it); only in the tray with -dev.
  • Even at pressure 0 it counts as an aura giver towards the aura cap; during the vent it does not.
  • A tender with no neighbours at all builds and vents anyway while dudes are on the table, to no effect.

Hooks, verbs and marks (from the code)

  • Hooks it overrides: Update, AuraFor
  • Engine verbs its code calls: After
